toṭakkattil milvaṭṭattil pōli paṭṭāḷam niṟuttappaṭṭatu; piṉṉē airōppiya irāṇuvam niṟuttap paṭṭatu. pōlisum irāṇuvamum aṇi aṇiyāka niṉṟu kāval purintamaiyāl ātitirāviṭar cilarum vēṟucilarum millukkuḷ cellalāyiṉar. avar kālai millukkuḷ pōkum pōtum, mālai tirumpum pōtum pōlisum irāṇuvamum purinta kāval kōlattai vēṭikkai pārkkac ceṉṉai vācikaḷ tiraṇṭu varuvārkaḷ.
āyirakkaṇakkāṉa toḻilāḷar millukkuḷ pōkātu virataṅ kāttamaiyāl veḷiyūrkaḷiliruntu āṭkaḷ tiraṭṭap paṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaṭkeṉat taṉiyiṭamum mil atikārikaḷāl amaikkappaṭṭatu.
nāḷukkunāḷ karuṅkālikaṭkum (Black Legs) vēlai niṟuttak kārarkkum palavitac caṇṭaikaḷ nikaḻntaṉa. katti veṭṭukkaḷum, veṭi vīccukkaḷum, vīṭukaḷil tī vaippum, piṟa mūrkkac ceyal kaḷum aṭikkaṭi naṭaipeṟṟaṉa.
kalakamum, kuḻappamum pala iṭaṅkaḷil paraviṉa. toḻilāḷar mītu vaḻakkukaḷum vāraṇṭukaḷum maliyalāyiṉa. caṅka nilaiyattai irāṇuvam kāval pūṇṭatu; nilaiyam cōtikkappaṭṭatu. caṅkattilē kālaiyilum mālaiyilum toḻilāḷarkku ahimsā tarma upatēcañ ceyyappaṭṭu vantatu.
nāṭu kaṭattal
yāṉ appoḻutu toḻilāḷar tollaiyil mūḻkiyirun tālum kāṅkira toṇṭai viṭuttēṉillai. tamiḻnāṭṭil cattiyākirakat toṇṭaic ceytē vantēṉ. cila iṭaṅkaḷil eṉṉuṭaṉ vantavarkkumaṭṭum 144 vaḻaṅkappaṭum; eṉakku vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatillai. aḵtu eṉakkum maṟṟavarkkum viyappūṭṭum. uriya kāraṇam palavāṟu collappaṭṭatu, palavita vatantikaḷ eḻuntu eḻuntu maṟaintaṉa. toḻilāḷar kuḻappaṅ kāraṇamāka aracāṅkam tiru.vi.ka.vai viḻuṅkappōkiṟatu. avarukkup perun taṇṭaṉai kiṭaikkap pōkiṟatu. ataṉāl avar pōkumiṭaṅ kaḷil 144 vaḻaṅkappaṭuvatillai eṉṟa vatanti maṭṭum nilaittu ulavalāyiṟṟu. ataṉai valiyuṟutta, lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ nīlakiriyiṉiṉṟum varappōkiṟār; vantatum tiru.vi.ka.vaiyum avaraic cārnta cilaraiyum nāṭu kaṭattap pōkiṟār eṉṟoru vatanti toṭarntu piṟantatu. atu kāṭṭut tīppōl ceṉṉai muḻuvatum paraviṟṟu.  naṇpar palarum eṉṉaik kaṇṭu kaṇṭu vicāritta vaṇṇamiruppar. akkālattil iraṭṭai āṭciyil pataviyil vīṟṟiruntavar jaṭi kaṭciyār.
lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ (4-7-1921) nīlakiriyiṉiṉṟum ceṉṉai cērntu mutalil karuṅkālikaḷaik kaṇṭaṉar; piṉṉē pala talaivar kaḷai varavaḻaittup pēciṉar.
aṭuttanāḷ mālai 6 maṇikkuk kavarṉar vīṭṭukku varumāṟu eṉakku aḻaippu vantatu. ataṟkiṇaṅki yāṉ kavarṉar māḷikaikkuc ceṉṟēṉ. eṉakku muṉṉarē tōḻarkaḷ cakkaraic ceṭṭiyār, i.el.aiyar, jalilkāṉ, aptul hakīm ākiya nālvar aṅkē pōntiruntaṉar. 6 maṇikku mantirimār valatu pakkamum nirvāka aṅkattavarkaḷ iṭatu pakkamum iruppa, lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ naṭuvilē amarntār. mēlnāṭṭu muṟaippaṭi mariyātaikaḷ naṭantaṉa. eṅkaḷ aivaraiyum lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ pārttu, 

toḻilāḷar vēlai niṟuttaṅ kāraṇamākac ceṉṉaiyil veṭṭuk kuttukkaḷum veṭikaḷum vīṭukaḷil tīvaippum naṭantu varukiṉṟaṉa. avaikaṭ kellām mūlakāraṇar nīṅkaḷ eṉṟu aṟikiṟēṉ. amaiti kākkum poṟuppu ṅkattukkuṇṭu...... eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. eṅkaḷil oruvar nāṅkaḷ kāraṇarallar eṉṟaṉar; maṟṟoruvar nītimaṉṟam irukkiṟatē eṉṟaṉar; iṉṉoruvar vicāraṇai vēṇṭum  eṉṟaṉar; vēṟoruvar toḻilāḷar caṅkattukkum eṉakkuñ campantamē illai. yāṉ tavaṟāka aḻaikkappaṭṭēṉ eṉṟaṉar; yāṉ, ellārkkum niyāyat tīrppunāḷ irukkiṟatu eṉṟēṉ. niyāyat tīrppu nāḷ eṉṟatu lārṭ villiṅṭaṉai uṟuttiyatu pōlum. lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ ēṟa iṟaṅkap pārttu, poḻutākiṟatu; yāṉ uṅkaḷai eccarikkai ceyyavē aḻaittēṉ. iṉit tīvaippu mutaliya _rkkacbcayalkaḷeṭakkumāṉālÚṅkaḷeṭufṭattappaṭuvīrkaḷvṉṟumḻuttamākamṟaintār.eh§fŸ Éṭaipeṟṟuåṭupōycncarntōm.m‹¿uî, ṭākṭar naṭēca mutaliyār eṉṉaik kaṇṭu, lārṭ villiṅṭaṉ eṅkaḷai nāṭukaṭatta uṟuti koṇṭē ceṉṉai nōkkiṉareṉṟum, avar sar.pi.tiyākarāja ceṭṭi yāraiyum talaimai amaiccar paṉakal rājāvaiyum kalantu ālōcitta pōtu, avviruvarum nāṭu kaṭattuñ ceyalāl tamatu kaṭcikkuk keṭṭa peyar uṇṭākumeṉṟum, ottuḻaiyāmaiyum malaiyāḷak kuḻappamum kaṉaṉṟu kotittuvarum vēḷaiyil nāṭu kaṭattalum nikaḻumāyiṉ nakarattil kuḻappam perukumeṉṟum, tām (paṉakal) pataviyiṉiṉṟum vilakalum nēralāmeṉṟum nilaimaiyai viḷakkiya piṉṉar eṅkaḷai eccarikkai ceytuviṭak kavarṉar eṇṇalāyiṉareṉṟum uraittār. ceṭṭiyārum paṉakalum eṉakku naṉmai ceyyavillai; tīmai ceytaṉar eṉṟu colli ṭākṭarai aṉuppiṉēṉ.
vaḻakkukaḷ
toḻilāḷar mītu pōlisārāl toṭarappeṟṟa vaḻakkukaḷai yellām virittuk kūṟutal anāvaciyam. iraṇṭu vaḻakkukkaḷiṉ tīrppukkaḷ (2,5-8-1921) pattirikaikaḷil virivāka veḷivantaṉa. attīrppukkaḷ potumakkaḷiṉ anutāpattait toḻilāḷar mītu tiruppiṉa. aniyāyam makkaḷukku naṉku viḷaṅkiṟṟu.
eyiliṅ kūṭṭa vicāraṇai
tī vaippu mutaliya mūrkkac ceyalkaḷai vicāraṇai puriya aracāṅkattāl oru kūṭṭam amaikkappaṭṭatu. talaivar jaṭi eyiliṅ; aṅkattavar iruvar. avar sar vēṅkaṭarattiṉamum karnūl vakkīl naracimmāccāriyārumāvar. vicāraṇai (10-8-1921il) toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. caṅkat talaivaṉ eṉṟa muṟaiyil cāṉṟu kūṟa yāṉ aḻaikkappaṭṭēṉ. avvaḻaippukku yāṉ iṇaṅka villai. iṇaṅkal iyalāteṉṟu patil viṭuttēṉ. atil kalaka nikaḻcci potumakkaḷaip poṟuttateṉṟum, ataṟkum ceṉṉait toḻilāḷar caṅkattukkum evvitat toṭarpum illai eṉṟum,  piratama pirasiṭeṉsi mājiṭirēṭṭāl aḷikkappeṟṟa iraṇṭu tīrppukkaḷāl kalaka mūlam naṉku viḷaṅkukiṟateṉṟum, iṉṉum cila vaḻakkukaḷ nītimaṉṟattil vicāraṇaiyiliruntu varukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum, eyiliṅ kūṭṭa vicāraṇaiyil kalantu koḷḷac caṅkam virumpavillai eṉṟum kuṟikkappeṟṟaṉa. 1eyiliṅ kamiṭṭiyār tamatu aṟikkaiyiṉ tōṟṟuvāyil oru cila kuṟippup poṟittu eṉatu kaṭitattaiyum veḷiyiṭṭaṉar.
tuppākki muḻakkam
ceṉṉai aracāṅkattiṉ ēvutaṟkiṇaṅkap pōlī kamiṣaṉar oru kaṭṭaḷai piṟappittaṉar. atu varumāṟu:-
ji. allatu vēppēri pōlī ṭēṣaṉ, ec. pōlī ṭēṣaṉaic cērnta mūlaik kottaḷam, viyācarpāṭi allatu vaṇṇārappēṭṭai pōlī ṭēṣaṉ ākiya ivvellaikkuḷ aṭaṅkiyirukkum jaṉaṅkaḷukkut terivippatu eṉṉa veṉṟāl:-
camīpattil naṭaipeṟṟa tī vipattukkaḷaip paṟṟiyum, amaitiyiṉmaiyaip paṟṟiyum aracāṅkattār peritum kavaṉittu varukiṉṟaṉar. aracāṅkattār immātiriyāṉa kuḻappaṅkaḷellām niṉṟu viṭutaṟkuriya kāriyaṅkaḷaiyum ceytu varukiṉṟaṉar. caṭṭattaiyum amaitiyaiyum nilai peṟuttutaṟkāka aracāṅkattār avaciyamāyc ceyyat takkaṉavaṟṟaic ceyvar.
caṭṭavirōtamāṉa ellāk kūṭṭaṅkaḷum kalaikkap paṭum. kalakattai uṇṭākka muyalkiṟavar mītum, āyutaṅkaḷ vaittirukkiṟavarmītum, kōpattai yuṇṭākkakkūṭiya ceykaikaḷaic ceykiṟavar mītum, aracāṅkattār kaṭumaiyāṉa muṟaikaḷaik kaiyāḷvar. aracāṅkattār tammāl kūṭiyavarai amaitiyā yirukkum jaṉaṅkaḷukkup pātukāppu aḷippar. avaciyam ēṟpaṭṭāl pātukāppuk kāka avviṭattil pōlīpaṭai niṟuttappaṭum. ataṉ celavai av viṭattiluḷḷavar koṭuttal vēṇṭum.
ikkaṭṭaḷaiyiṉ uḷḷak kiṭakkai uḷḷaṅkai nellik kaṉiyeṉa viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
uṇaviṉṟip paṭṭiṉiyāl vāṭṭamuṟṟuk kiṭanta toḻi lāḷarkkut tuppākki uṇavu - kuṇṭu uṇavu - aḷikkappaṭṭatu (29-8-1921) ēḻait toḻilāḷar mītu tuppākkip pirayōkañ ceyyappaṭṭatu. eḻuvar iṟantaṉar; nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉavar kāya muṟṟaṉar. iṟantavaruḷ oruvar peṇmaṇi, tuppākki vēṭṭiṉiṭai avar purinta vīrac ceyalukkuriya niṉaivukkuṟi eṉṟu ceṉṉai amaikkumō aṟikilēṉ. ēḻaimakkaḷ tiyākam maṟakkappaṭukiṟatu.  ēḻu piṇaṅkaḷum ceṉṉaiyil ūrvalam vanta kāṭciyait tōḻar ciṅkāravēl ceṭṭiyār varuṇittu eḻutiya kaṭṭurai carittira ulakukkuriyatu. mēlum tuppākkip pirayōkaṅ kaḷ akṭōpar 15lum, cepṭampar 19lum nikaḻntaṉa. iruvar māṇṭaṉar; cilar kāya muṟṟaṉar.
pōlisār cārpil uyir tuṟantavar iruvar. oruvar heṭkāṉṭepil; maṟṟoruvar cārjeṇṭ. avviruvarum toḻilāḷar eṟikuṇṭāl kollappaṭṭaṉar eṉṟu collap paṭṭatu. acceyti uṟutippaṭavillai.
kuḻappa nikaḻccikaḷ pala eṉṉuḷḷattiṉiṉṟum mūṇṭeḻuntu varukiṉṟaṉa. avaikaṭkeṉa oru taṉinūl eḻututal vēṇṭum. kuḻappattaip paṟṟi nakara paripālaṉa capaiyilum, caṭṭa capaiyilum vivātaṅkaḷ naṭantaṉa. avaikaḷil tiru. o.taṇikācalam ceṭṭiyār pēciya 1pēccukkaḷil pala uṇmaikaḷ curuṅkiya muṟaiyil viḷaṅkik koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
muṭivu
vēlai niṟuttam eppaṭi muṭintatu? rājā sar. irāmacāmi mutaliyār paṅkaḷā veḷiyil kūṭiya kūṭṭattil sar.pi.tiyākarāja ceṭṭiyār nikaḻttiya irāja tantirac coṟperukkil mūḻkit toḻilāḷar palar vēlaikkut tirumpiṉar. sar.pi.ceṭṭiyār, mātaṅkaḷ āṟāyiṉa. iṉip piṭivātam vēṇṭā. uṅkaḷukku irāja tantiram vēṇṭum. milkaḷil putu āṭkaḷ nirappappaṭu kiṟārkaḷ. putu āṭkaḷ toḻilaip payiṉṟu viṭuvārkaḷāyiṉ uṅkaḷukkuk kaṣṭam viḷaiyum. pōli kāvalaiyum, irāṇuvak kāvalaiyuṅ kaṭantu avarkaḷait taṭuttal iyalātu. āṉāl avarkaḷai oru vaḻiyil appuṟap paṭuttal kūṭum. nīṅkaḷellārum vēlaikkuc cella uṟutik koṇṭāl avarkaḷ vīṭṭukkaṉuppap paṭuvārkaḷ. ātalāl, nīṅkaḷ uḷḷē pōṅkaḷ. naṉmaiyē viḷaiyum. caṅkam cākātu eṉṟu pēciya pēccu av vēḷaiyil toḻilāḷarkku ucitamākat tōṉṟiṟṟu. sar.pi.ceṭṭi yār jōciyam peritum palittatu.
iraṇṭu
toḻilāḷar iyakkattai muḷaiyilēyē kiḷḷi eṟiyac ceyyappeṟṟa cūḻccikaḷuḷ ciṟantaṉavāka uḷḷa iraṇṭu iṅkuk kuṟikkappeṟṟaṉa. oṉṟu vaḻakku; maṟṟoṉṟu nāṭu kaṭattum eccarikkai. piṉṉaiyatiṉ aṭippaṭai ilāpattil mikac ciṟu urimai toḻilāḷarkku vēṇṭumeṉṟu pēcappaṭṭamai; muyaṉṟamai aṉṟu, veṟum pēccukku ettuṇai ettuṇai etirppu! etirppāl vitai paṭṭuppōy viṭṭatō? vitai muḷaiviṭṭē varukiṟatu. ataṟkuriya oḷiyum kāṟṟum nīrum paṭarntu, vīci, pāyntē varukiṉṟaṉa. avvoḷiyaiyum kāṟṟai yum nīraiyum vaṟṟac ceyyum āṟṟal etaṟku millai; evarkku millai.
intiyāvil mutal mutal tōṉṟiya ceṉṉait toḻilāḷar caṅka nikaḻccikaḷ iṉṉuñ cila uḷa. avaṟṟai innūṟkaṇ virippatu poruttamākātu. avaṟṟai eṉṉāl iyaṟṟap peṟṟa tiru.vi.ka. vāḻkkaik kuṟippukkaḷ eṉṉum nūliṟ kāṇka. 1947ām āṇṭu nikaḻcci carittirattil patiyattakkatu. ataṟkeṉa oru taṉi nūl eḻutappaṭalām.
toḻiṟ kāṅkira
ciṟu ciṟu muyaṟcikaḷ oṉṟupaṭṭu araciyal viṭutalaikkuk kāṅkirasāka mukiḻttamai pōlat toḻiṟ caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ ciṟu ciṟu muyaṟcikaḷ oṉṟi oṉṟi akila intiyat toḻiṟcaṅkak kāṅkirasāka mukiḻttaṉa. mutal kāṅkira (31-10-1920) lālā lajapatirāy talaimaiyil pampāyil kūṭiyatu.
1929ām āṇṭil toḻiṟ caṅkak kāṅkira paṇṭita javaharlāl nēru talaimaiyil kūṭiṟṟu. akkūṭṭattil piḷavu ēṟpaṭṭatu. oru kūṭṭam mitavātattil cikkuṇṭatu. maṟṟoṉṟu camatarmattiṉ vayappaṭṭatu. ippoḻutu mitavātam kuṟaintum kulaintum varukiṟatu.
taṟkāla nilai
toḻiṟcaṅka iyakkam tōṉṟi ēṟakkuṟaiya muppatu āṇṭuka ḷākiṉṟaṉa. ittuṇai nāḷ evvaḷavō kāriyaṅkaḷaic cātit tirukkalām. iyakkam toṭakkattil naṉṉilaiyiliruntatu. piṉṉē nāḷukku nāḷ iṟukalāyiṟṟu. annāḷil talaivarka ḷukkuḷ oṟṟumai nilaviyatu; avarkaḷāl naṭattappeṟṟa caṅkaṅ kaḷukkum oṟṟumai nilaviyatu; piṉṉē talaivarkaḷukkuḷ piṇakku eḻuntatu; caṅkaṅkaḷilum piṇakku eḻuntatu.
araciyal amaippukkaḷil toḻiṟcaṅkaṅkaḷukkeṉṟum toḻilāḷarkkeṉṟum cila pīṭaṅkaḷ otukkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟil talaivarkaḷ amartaṟkuc ceyyappaṭum muyaṟcikaḷil avarkaḷuk kuḷ piṇakku nērum. appiṇakkuc caṅkaṅkaḷilum pukuntu avaṟṟai nācappaṭuttum.
patavi māyai caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ oṟṟumaiyaik kulaittatōṭu maṟṟoru māyaiyum avaṟṟiṉ oṟṟumaiyaik kulaittatu. atu jiṉivā māyai. jiṉivā cellat talaivarkaḷukkuḷ pōṭṭi eḻum. appōṭṭiyāl viḷaiyum maṉattāṅkalkaḷ caṅkaṅ kaḷait tākkum.
toḻilāḷar caṅkaṅkaḷ peritum patavi māyaikkum jiṉivā māyaikkum iraiyāyiṉa. ammāyaikaḷiṉ poruṭṭuc caṅkaṅ kaḷ ōmpappaṭṭaṉa. toḻilāḷar nalaṉukkeṉṟu caṅkaṅkaḷ ōmpappaṭuvatu arumaiyākiyatu.
talaivarkaḷ oruvarōṭoruvar kalantu vāḻa virumpukiṉ ṟārillai; taṉittaṉiyē vāḻa virumpukiṉṟaṉar. it talaivar kaḷaik koṇṭa caṅkaṅkaḷukkuḷ eṅṅaṉam oṟṟumai uṇṭākum? mutalāḷikaḷ koṭumaiyālum - aracāṅka aṭakku muṟaiyālum - kulaiyāta caṅkaṅkaḷum, talaivarkaḷ piṇakkāl kulaiyuṅ kāṭciyai vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
kāṅkira āṭci
kāṅkira āṭci toḻiṟ kāṅkirasukku ākkan tēṭa villai; atai oṭukkavē muyalkiṟatu eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ippoḻutu akila intiya tēciya kāṅkira eṉṟōr amaippu vallapāy paṭṭēl ācipeṟṟut tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu. atu paḻaiya toḻiṟ kāṅkirasai aḻippataṟkeṉṟu tōṉṟiyateṉpatu paṭēliṉ pēccāl uṇaralākum. paḻaiya toḻiṟ kāṅkirasil potu uṭaimaik kaṭci ākkam peṟṟuḷḷamaiyāl, putut toḻiṟ kāṅkira tōṟṟattukku avaciyam nērntateṉṟu paṭēl pēciṉar. putiya amaippu, kāṅkira kāriyak kūṭṭattiṉ ātaravaiyum peṟṟuviṭṭatu. kāṅkirakārar paḻaiya amaippai vēṭṭaiyāṭat tuvaṅkiyuḷḷaṉar. putu amaippu, mutalāḷikaḷ cārpil tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭateṉṟu nāṭu muḻuvatum pēcap paṭukiṟatu.
iḷaiñar 
innerukkaṭiyāṉa nērattil poruḷātāra nuṭpattai naṉku uṇarnta iḷaiñarkaḷ toḻiṟ caṅkaṅkaḷil cērntu varukiṟārkaḷ. avarkaḷāl poruḷātāra nuṭpam piracārañ ceyyappaṭukiṟatu. iṉit toḻilāḷarkaḷ nalluṇarvu peṟṟu oṟṟumaiyuṭaiyavarkaḷākalām.
vivacāyac caṅkam
toḻilāḷar iyakkam iṉṉum nāṭṭil naṉku parava villai. aḵtu ippoḻutu toḻiṟcālaikaḷil vēlai ceyvōraik koṇṭa caṅka aḷavil niṟkiṟatu. atu mēlum maṟṟa maṟṟat toḻilāḷariṭaṅkaḷilum ūṭuruvip pāytal vēṇṭum; ciṟappāka vivacāyikaḷiṭattil pukuvatu nallatu.
namatu nāṭṭil perumpāṉmaiyōr vivacāyikaḷ. avarkaḷē nāṭṭiṉ mutukelumpāyiruppavarkaḷ. aṟarkaḷai mikac cilar oṭukki āṇṭu varukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ viṭutalai, nāṭṭiṉ viṭutalaiyākum; poruḷātāra viṭutalaiyākum, ākavē poruḷātārap piracāram peritum vivacāyikaḷiṭaip paravutal vēṇṭum. vivacāyikaḷiṭaip poruḷātāra ñāṉam perukum nāḷē nāṭṭukkuriya naṉṉāḷeṉka. vivacāyikaḷukkup poruḷā tāra ñāṉattai yūṭṭātu, avarkaḷiṭai ataiyum itaiyum pēci, veṟum aṟivaṟṟa āvēcattai eḻuppuvatu avarkaḷai ēmāṟṟuvatākum. 
innāḷil vaṭa intiyāvil uḻavar caṅkaṅkaḷ, ākkam peṟṟu varukiṉṟaṉa. teṉṉintiyāvilum accaṅkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi varukiṉṟaṉa. nāṭu muḻuvatum uḻavar caṅkam perukuvatu ciṟappu. uḻavar iyakkattai oṭukka nāṭṭuk kāṅkira aracu muṉaintu niṟkiṟatu. potujaṉap pātukāppuc caṭṭameṉṟu oṉṟu niṟuvappeṟṟuḷḷatu. atu raulaṭ caṭṭattiṉuṅ koṭiyatu. accaṭṭam toḻiliyakkat talaivarkaḷai viḻuṅki varukiṟatu. kāṅkira āṭci mutalāḷi āṭciyāki varutal varuntattakkatu.
kāṅkirasil toḻiliyakkam
iṉit toḻiliyakkam caṅka aḷavil niṟṟalākātu. toḻiṟcaṅka iyakkam toḻiliyakkattiṉ oru paṭi, toḻiliyakkam appaṭiyilēyē niṉṟu viṭṭāl ataṉāl oṉṟum ceytal iyalātu. toḻiliyakkam kāṅkira iyakkamātal vēṇṭum; nāṭṭiyakkamātal vēṇṭum; āyiṉ poruḷātāra viṭutalai tāṉē uṇṭākum.
nāṭṭiṉ viṭutalaikkeṉṟu ēṟpaṭṭatu kāṅkira. kāṅkira viṭutalaikkup pāṭupaṭṭatu. nāṭu anniya āṭciyiṉiṉṟum viṭutalai aṭaintatu. itu muḻu viṭutalaiyākumā? anniya āṭciyiṉiṉṟu maṭṭum viṭutalai aṭaintu, oru ciṟu kūṭṭam iṉpuṟa, maṟṟoru peruṅkūṭṭattai vēlaiyiṉṟit tiṇṭāṭac ceytu, paṭṭiṉiyālum paciyālum atait tuṉpuṟuttuvatu muḻu viṭutalaiyākātu. muḻu viṭutalaiyāvatu, anniya āṭciyiṉiṉṟum viṭutalai aṭaivatuṭaṉ vēlaiyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam eppakutiyilum tōṉṟāmal nāṭṭāraṉaivarum ētō oruvitat toḻil ceytu nalvāḻkkai naṭātti iṉpuṟat tuṇaipurivatu. itaṟkuc celvam oru pakkam peruki maṟṟoru pakkam arukāta vāṟu kākkavalla āṭcimuṟai tēvai. atuvē camatarma āṭci eṉpatu.
muṉṉē kāṅkira āṭci pala mākāṇaṅkaḷil iraṇṭarai āṇṭu naṭaipeṟṟatu. akkālattil mantirimār camatarma āṭci kālkoḷḷavāvatu muyaṉṟaṉarā? cāmrājya āṭcimuṟaiyai ōmpuvatilēyē avartam muyaṟci ceṉṟatu. avartam āṭciyil vēlaiyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam perukiṟṟā? arukiṟṟā? nāṭu patiliṟuppatāka, mīṇṭum kāṅkira, āṭci ēṟṟatu. eṉṉa ceytatu? potujaṉap pātukāppeṉṟu urimaiyaik kallavalla oru koṭuñ caṭṭañ ceytatu. ataṉāl camatarmattai niṟuvavalla toḻiliyakkam paṭum pāṭṭai nāṭaṟiyum.
ippoḻutu nāṭu oruvita viṭutalaiyaṭaintatu. atai muḻu viṭutalaiyākkum muyaṟci nāṭṭil eḻutal vēṇṭum. muḻu viṭutalaikku nāṭu camatarmamayamātal vēṇṭum. kāṅkirasum toḻiliyakkamum oṉṟiṉālaṉṟi nāṭu camatarma mayamākātu. ākavē, toḻilāḷar iyakkattaik kāṅkirasil oṉṟac ceyya nāṭṭavar muyalvārāka.
camatarmam
camatarmam eṉṟatuñ cilar añcukiṉṟaṉar; cilar ataip puli karaṭi ciṅkameṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar. atu tavaṟu. camatarma tattuvattaip pakuttaṟivāl ārāyntu cintittu oru muṭivukku varutalē aṟivuṭaimai. camatarma virōtikaḷ - cuyanalakkārarkaḷ - piracārattai maṭṭuṅ kēṭṭu añcuvatu aṟivuṭaimaiyākātu.
camatarmattaip paṟṟi itukāṟum palatiṟak karuttukkaḷ veḷivantuḷḷaṉa. akkaruttukkaḷaṭaṅkiya nūlkaḷ peritum mēl nāṭṭavarāl iyaṟṟappaṭṭavai. intiya aṟiñar cilarum camatarmattaip paṟṟip pēciyum eḻutiyum varukiṉṟaṉar. appēccum eḻuttum peritum mēlnāṭṭu nulkaḷiṉ etirolikaḷākavē irukkiṉṟaṉa. veṟum nūlulakil maṭṭum ulavi oru nāṭṭait tirutta muyalvatāl nalam viḷaiyātu. cila camayam tīmai viḷaiyiṉum viḷaiyum.
āṉṟōriṭattiruntu kālattukkēṟṟa tattuvam piṟakkum. attattuvattaiyoṭṭi avvannāṭṭu iyaṟkai araṇ ceyyum muṟaiyil tiṭṭaṅ kōlic cīrtiruttañ ceyvatu araciyal ñāṉamākum. oru nāṭṭu muṟai maṟṟoru nāṭṭu iyaṟkaikkup poruntiyumirukkum; poruntāmalumirukkum; orupuṭaip poruntiyum maṟṟoru puṭaip poruntāmalumirukkum. oṉṟai nāṭṭiṭai nuḻaippataṟku muṉṉar, atu nāṭṭiṉ iyaṟkaikkup porunti varumā vārātā eṉṟu ārāyntu teḷinta piṉṉarē atai nuḻaikka muyalvatu aṟivukku aḻaku.
paḻamperuṅ kālattil ulakam iyaṟkaiyōṭiyaintu vāḻntamaiyāl atu camatarma vaḻiyil iyaṅki vantatu. piṉṉē ceyaṟkai vāḻkkai mutira mutirac camatarmam cāyntu cāmrājyam eḻalāyiṟṟu. ikkāla ulakam cāmrājya āṭciyāl aluttuk kaḷaittu nalintu melintu piṇipaṭṭut tuṉpuṟukiṟatu. ataṉāl ulakam paḻaiya camatarmattai nāṭā niṟkiṟatu.
paḻaṅkālattil nilam makkaḷukkup potuvākavē payaṉ paṭṭu vantatu. nāḷaṭaivil ataṉ potumai māṟi atu taṉi maṉitar uṭaimaiyāyiṟṟu. perumpāṉmaiyōrkku nila urimai aṟṟup pōyviṭṭatu. aracarkaḷum, jamīṉtārikaḷum, mirācutārar kaḷum, celvarkaḷum nilamuṭaiyavarkaḷāṉārkaḷ. maṟṟavar avarkaḷiṭattil vēlai ceytu kūlipeṟṟup piḻaippavarāyiṉar.
paṇṭai nāḷil makkaḷ kaittoḻilkaḷ ceytu vāḻntu vantārkaḷ. annāḷil mutalāḷi toḻilāḷi vēṟṟumai eḻa villai. mutal toḻil aṟṟa camūkamē nilaviyatu. piṉṉē iyantira kālan tōṉṟiṟṟu. iyantirattāl uṟpatti atikam eṉṟu colla vēṇṭuvatillai. mutalāḷikaḷ iyantiraṅkaḷaik koṇṭu uṟpattikaḷaip perukkat taṅkaṭkut tēvaiyāṉa āṭkaḷai amarttik kūlikoṭuttu varalāṉārkaḷ. uṟpattic cātaṉaṅka ḷellām mutalāḷi vayappaṭṭuk kiṭattal kaṇkūṭu.
īriṉam
ippoḻutu ulakiluḷḷa makkaḷ iṉattai iru kūṟākap pirikkalām. oṉṟu mutalāḷi iṉam; maṟṟoṉṟu toḻilāḷi iṉam. iraṇṭil mutalāḷi iṉam āḷvatāyum, toḻilāḷi iṉam āḷappaṭuvatāyum irukkiṉṟaṉa.
mutalāḷi āṭcikku muṉṉē pirapukkaḷ āṭci naṭai peṟṟatu. pirapukkaḷ āṭcikku muṉṉē maṉṉarāṭci naṭai peṟṟatu. maṉṉarāṭci eppaṭip piṟantatu?
ovvoru kuṭumpattilum ovvoru talaivar iruntaṉar. kuṭumpaṅkaḷ cērnta oṉṟu kirāmam. kuṭumpat talaivarellāñ cērntu kirāmat talaivarait terinteṭuttaṉar. kirāmaṅka ḷālāyavai kōṭṭam mutaliyaṉa. kirāmat talaivarkaḷ oṉṟu paṭṭuk kōṭṭam mutaliyavaṟṟukkut talaivarkaḷai ēṟpaṭutti ṉārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ kūṭit taṅkaḷukku oru talaivarai niyamittuk koṇṭārkaḷ. avarē maṉṉarāyiṉar.
ittalaivar palarum, toṭakkattil kaṭavuḷiṭattu uṟutiyum aṉpum uṭaiyavarāy, jīvakāruṇya muṭaiyavarāy, tamakkeṉa vāḻātu maṟṟavarkkeṉa vāḻvōrāy, nila pulaṅkaḷait tarmakarttarāka niṉṟu kāppavarāy, viḷaivu ellārkkum payaṉpaṭat tuṇai ceyvōrāyt toṇṭāṟṟi vantaṉar. nāḷaṭaivil talaivarkaḷ nilaimai māṟiviṭṭatu. avarkaḷ nilapulaṅkaḷai atikārattāl taṅkaḷuṭaimaikaḷākkik koṇ ṭārkaḷ. nāḷukkunāḷ maṉṉarāṭciyil koṭuṅkōl pukuntatu eṉṟu curuṅkac collalām. avvāṭci cāykkap paṭṭatu. evarāl? pirapukkaḷāl. pirapukkaḷ āṭci, mutalāḷikaḷāl kaviḻkkappaṭṭatu. cila kālamāka mutalāḷi āṭci cāmrājyamāyt talaivirittāṭukiṟatu.
cāmrājyattai vīḻttuṅ kiḷarcciyun tōṉṟiyuḷḷatu ēṉ? iyaṟkaiyai nōkkik kēṭṭal vēṇṭum. cāmrājyattāl ulakam peṟṟuvarum anupavam eṉṉa? kuḻappam eṉṟu oru  collāl patiliṟukkalām.
iyaṟkaik kūṟukaḷellām potumaiyil makkaḷukkup payaṉpaṭṭu varutal kaṇkūṭu. potumai, makkaḷukkuḷ kuḻappam nikaḻātavāṟu kāttu varuntaṉmai vāyntatu. ākavē, iyaṟkaik kūṟukaḷiṉ nōkkattukkut tuṇai ceyyavalla aṟa āṭci naṭaipeṟutal vēṇṭum. illaiyēl, maṟakkuḻappam nikaḻntu koṇṭē irukkum. potumai aṟattukku māṟupaṭṭa āṭci yāl viḷaintuvarun tīṅkukaḷaip palapaṭak kūṟalām. avaṟṟuḷ mūṉṟu ciṟappākak kuṟikkattakkaṉa. oṉṟu vēlaiyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam; iṉṉoṉṟu pōrāṭṭam; maṟṟoṉṟu oḻukkak kēṭu.
nila pulaṅkaḷum toḻiṟcālaikaḷum maṟṟa uṟpatti tāpaṉaṅ kaḷum taṉi maṉitar uṭaimaiyāṉapaṭiyāl, avar tam toḻiṉ muṟaikaṭkut tēvaiyāṉa āṭkaḷai maṭṭum vaittu vēlai vāṅkuvōrāyiṉar. maṟṟavarkku vēlai kiṭaippatillai. vēlai yillāt tiṇṭāṭṭattāl paci paṭṭiṉi pañcam akāla maraṇam kuḻappam mutaliyaṉa nikaḻtal iyalpu. vēlaiyillātār kiḷarcciyai oṭukkutaṟkeṉṟu mutalāṭciyiṉ caṭṭaṅkaḷum, pōlisum, irāṇuvamum, pattirikaikaḷum, iṉṉa piṟavum kāttuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa.
nila pulaṅkaḷum piṟavum potumai aṟa āṭciyiṉ uṭaimai yāyiṉ, vēlaiyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭattukku iṭam uṇṭākumō? uṇṭākātu. ulakil piṟanta cilarē vāḻtal vēṇṭumeṉpatā iyaṟkaiyiṉ nōkkam? ellārum naṉku vāḻtal vēṇṭu meṉpatē iyaṟkaiyiṉ nōkkam. ataṟkut tuṇaipurital mutalāḷi āṭciyāl iyalavillaiyeṉpatu veḷḷiṭai malaiyeṉa viḷaṅkiviṭṭatu. attakaiya āṭcimuṟai māṟutal vēṇṭumā vēṇṭāmā? māṟutal vēṇṭum eṉṟu colvōr mutalāḷi kūṭṭattiṉ pakaivarallar; ellārum iṉpuṟṟu vāḻa vēṇṭumeṉṉum jīvakāruṇyamuṭaiyavar. avar evariṭattum pakaimai koḷḷātavar. mutalāḷi āṭciyil camatarmam nilava iṭamiṉmaiyāl, atu māṟuvatu nalam eṉṟu colvatu aṟamēyākum.
iyantiraṅkaḷ, uṟpattiyaip perukkuvatāl mutalāḷi iṉattai ācaippēy alaikkap pukukiṟatu. ācaippēyiṉiṉṟum viṭutalaiyaṭaital avviṉattāl iyalavillai. ācaikkōr aḷavum uṇṭō? iyantira uṟpattikaḷ nāṭṭiṉ tēvaikku mēl perukik kuvikiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl piṟanāṭṭu vāṇipa nāṭṭamum uṭaṉ perukukiṟatu. iyantira uṟpatti ōṅkiyuḷḷa ovvoru nāṭṭu mutalāḷi varkkattukkum, iyantira vācaṉai aṟiyā nāṭukaḷai - kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷai - paṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṉum ācai uṇṭākiṟatu. avvācai, vāṉ pōr - nilappōr - nīrp pōrāka mūḷkiṟatu. ācaiyē kuṇṭu pīraṅkiyāy, naccukkāṟṟāy, piṟa kolaik karuvikaḷāyp pariṇamirukkiṟatu. ennēramum caṇṭai nikaḻnta vaṇṇamirukkiṟatu. ettaṉaic carva tēca caṅkaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉum, avai caṇṭaiyai niṟuttum vallamai peṟāmalē oḻikiṉṟaṉa. aṭippaṭai mutalāḷi varkkattiṉ ācai.
kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭukaḷ iyantira mutalāḷi varkkattukku iraiyāṉatum, annāṭukaḷil kālaṅ kālamāka vaḷarntuvanta kaittoḻilkaḷ nācamākiṉṟaṉa. nācamākum nilaimai mutalāḷi ācaiyāl ēṟpaṭukiṟatu. iyantiram vēṇṭāmeṉpatu eṉatu karuttaṉṟu; iyantiram potumai āṭciyilēyē makkaḷaṉai varukkum nalam purivatākumeṉpatum, cāmrājya āṭciyil iyantiram cilarkku nalaṉum palarkkuk kēṭum viḷaikku meṉpatum, potumai āṭciyil iyantiram ellārkkum nalam purivatākumeṉpatum eṉatu karuttu.
cāmrājya āṭcimuṟai vaḷarntu vantāl ulakam eṉṉākum? cintikka. caṇṭai oḻital vēṇṭumā vēṇṭāmā eṉṟu āḻntu cintikka. caṇṭaiyoḻiya vēṇṭumāṉāl cāmrājya āṭcimuṟai oḻital vēṇṭumaṉṟō? caṇṭai vēṇṭāmeṉṟu colvatu pāvamā?
mutalāḷi āṭciyil vēlaiyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam perukuvatāl maṉpataikkaṇ oḻukkam keṭukiṟatu. vēlaiyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam āṇulakai maṭṭum pīṭippatillai; peṇ ṇulakaiyum pīṭikkiṟatu. ataṉāl tīyoḻukkam paravukiṟatu. iccaikkāka vipacāram ceyvōr tokai ciṟiyatu; mikacciṟiyatu. vaṟumaiyāl vayiṟṟukkāka vipacāram ceyvōr tokai periya tākiyatu; mikap periyatākiṟatu. vēlaiyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam tīyoḻukkattaiyum vaḷarkkiṟatu. paṇattimirāl viḷaiyum tīyoḻukkaṅkaḷ kōṭi kōṭi ātaliṉ, oḻukkattiṉ poruṭṭum cāmrājya āṭci muṟai oḻital vēṇṭumeṉka.
takka maruntu
cāmrājya āṭciyai vaittuk koṇṭē vēlaiyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭattai oḻippateṉpatum, yuttattai niṟuttuva teṉpatum, oḻukkattai ōmpuvateṉpatum ēmāṟṟamēyākum. ēmāṟṟattukku ulakam iraiyātal kūṭātu.
cāmrājya āṭci muṟaiyilēyē vēlaiyillāt tiṇṭāṭ ṭattai oḻikkavum, pōrāṭṭattai niṟuttavum, oḻukkattai ōmpavum palappala muṟaikaḷ aṟiñarāl kōlappaṭṭaṉa. avai cōtikkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟāl etirpārtta nalaṉ viḷaiyavillai. ākavē, vēlaiyillāt tiṇṭāṭṭam mutaliya nōykaḷaip pōkka ētēṉum takka maruntu kāṇappaṭutal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? nerukkaṭi mutira mutira vaḻituṟai viḷaṅkik koṇṭē pōkum. palappala kāṇappaṭṭu muṭivil paḻaiya cama tarmamē marunteṉṟu aṟiñarāl koḷḷappaṭṭatu. paḻaiya camatarmattai viññāṉat tuṇai koṇṭu putukka vēṇṭuvatu ikkāla aṟiñar kaṭamai.
peyar?
āṭcimuṟaip peyaraip paṟṟiya kavalai vēṇṭuvatillai. eppeyarāyiṉumāka; evvāṭcimuṟai āyiṉumāka atu, makkaḷ ellārum vēlai ceytu piḻaikkavum tīṅku viḷaikkum pōrāṭṭamiṉṟi vāḻavum, oḻukkattai ōmpavum tuṇai ceyyavallatā yiruttal vēṇṭum. avvāṭcimuṟai camatarmam eṉṟu aḻaikkappaṭukiṟatu. atai eppeyarāl aḻaittā leṉṉa? tattuvam maṭṭum piṟaḻātiruttal vēṇṭum.
camatarmam eṉpatu putiyataṉṟu. atu paṇṭaik kālattil āṭciyiliruntatē. aḵtu ovvoru pōtu ovvoru vitamākak koḷḷappaṭṭu vantatu. attarmam ikkālac cāmrājya āṭcimuṟaiyāl maṟaintu pōyiṟṟu. ippoḻutu ulakam cāmrājyattāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
mārk
cāmrājyattāl viḻuṅkappaṭṭa ulakai, viṭutalai ceytu, camatarmattāl cīrtirutti amaikkap piṉṉāḷil muyaṉṟavar cilar. avaruḷ ciṟantavar kāral mārk. camatarmattai viññāṉa muṟaiyāl oḻuṅkupaṭutti ulakukkīnta perumai kāral mārksukku uṇṭu. mārk pōtaṉaiyaic cātaṉaiyākka muyaṉṟavar leṉiṉ uḷḷiṭṭa cilar.
mārksiyam ulakeṅkum innāḷil ārāyappaṭukiṟatu; cintikkappaṭukiṟatu. aḵtu iṉṉum yāṇṭum muṟṟum nilavic ceykaiyil nikaḻavillai. ruṣyāvil maṭṭum mārksiya vitai viḻuntirukkiṟatu. aṅkē attattuvam cōtaṉaiyiliruntu varukiṟatu.
mārksiyattaippaṟṟi muṉṉarum eṉ karuttai veḷiyiṭ ṭuḷḷēṉ. namatu intiya nāṭṭil mārksiyam emmuṟaiyil vaḷarum eṉpatu paṟṟiyum āṅkē kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷēṉ; mēlum atupaṟṟik kūṟuvēṉ.
āti manuvum tiripum
mārksiyam tōṉṟutaṟkup pallāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉṉarē namatu nāṭṭil ātimanu (mirutimanu alla) eṉpavar tōṉṟi maṉpataikkut tarumanūl tantaṉar. attaruma nūlil piṉṉē. iṭai iṭai avvakkālattuk kēṟṟavāṟu, ciṟcila māṟṟaṅkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. ātimanuvāl tarumattiṉ kūṟupāṭu kaḷākak kōlappeṟṟa toḻiṉ muṟaikaḷ piṉṉarp piṟappu vaḻiyil nikaḻumāṟu tirikkappaṭṭaṉa. attiripuṭaṉ mēlum mēlum piṟappil uyarvu tāḻvu kaṟpikkappaṭṭaṉa. attiripum ik kaṟpaṉaiyum intiyāvai aṭimaippaṭuttiṉa. ikkuṟaipāṭukaḷai nīkki, ātimanuviṉ tarmattai nōkkiṉāl, atu mārksiyat tukkut tāyakamākavē viḷaṅkutal kāṇalām.
kāral mārk jermaṉiyil piṟantavar (1818 - 1883). avar pala kalai payiṉṟu oru peruṅkaḻakamāyiṉar. avaratu paranta kalaiñāṉam avarkkoru putumai oḷiyai vaḻaṅkiyatu. atu potumai oḷi. avvoḷiyiṉiṉṟum eḻunta eṇṇamum, pēccum, eḻuttum, muyaṟciyum akkāla jermeṉ aracai naṭukkuṟac ceytaṉa. naṭukkam mārksai nāṭṭai viṭuttōṭumāṟu turattiyatu. peljiyam, pirāṉ mutaliya nāṭukaḷum avarait taṅkaḷiṭam taṅkaviṭavillai. avaiyum avaraik kaṭintu muṭukkiṉa. iṅkilāntu mārk vatiya iṭantantatu aṅkē avar vaṟumaik kalaiyil mūḻkiṉar; ulaka vaṟumaip piṇiyaiyum, ataṟku aṭippaṭaiyāyuḷḷa aṟiyāmai nōyaiyum pōkka muyaṉṟaṉar; muyaṉṟu takka maruntu kaṇṭaṉar. pala maruttuvak kalaikaḷ avarpāl malarntaṉa. avarāl ulakukkup potumai aṟivuṟuttappaṭṭatu. ataṉuṭaṉ namatu nāṭṭup paṇṭai aṟamum nuḻaintu oṉṟiṉāl, ulakam poruḷiṉpattuṭaṉ amaiti iṉpattaiyum nukarvatākum.
amaiti
cāmrājyaṅkaḷukkuḷ nikaḻum pōrāṭṭam perukap peruka makkaḷukkuḷ teḷivu ēṟpaṭum. atteḷivukkut tuṇai ceyvatu mārksiyam. mārksiyattāl ellārum poruḷiṉpattai nukarum nilai nēriṉum. amaiti iṉpattai nukarum nilai nērātu. poruḷiṉpattiṉūṭē aṟamum kalantu niṟṟal vēṇṭum. aṟaṅkalanta poruḷiṉpamē amaitiyai nilaipeṟutta vallatu. ataṟkuriya tuṟaikaḷ intiya aṟavōr palarāl naṉku kōlappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. ākavē, atu mēlum tarumattai avāviyē cīrpaṭum.
muḻu viṭutalai
tarumattukku intiyā vaḻi kāṭṭutal vēṇṭum. itaṟku intiyā ippoḻutu peṟṟuḷḷa viṭutalai pōtātu. atu muḻuviṭutalai peṟutal vēṇṭum. muḻu viṭutalaikku aṭippaṭai etu? poruḷātāra viṭutalai. ituvē camatarmameṉpatu.
camatarmattukkuk kāṅkira iṇaṅkumā eṉpatu oru peruṅkēḷvi. kāṅkira ippōtu mutalāḷi vayappaṭṭiruk kiṟatu. poṟuppāṭci poṟuppāṭci eṉṟu eḻuppappeṟṟa muḻakkamellām paṇakkārarkkuc cātakamāyiṉa; ēḻai makkaṭkup pātakamāyiṉa.
cātaka pātakam eṉṉa ceykiṉṟaṉa? mutal - toḻil pōrait tuvaṅkac ceykiṉṟaṉa. muṉṉē ippōr valukkavillai. kāraṇam, anniyam āṭciyiliruntamaiyē. annāḷil anniya āṭciyiṉiṉṟum viṭutalai peṟutaṟkē kāṅkira toḻilāḷi utaviyai nāṭiyatu. toḻilāḷi kāṅkirasukkut tuṇaipōṉāṉ; maṉamārat tuṇaipōṉāṉ. ataṉāl anniya āṭci tolaintatu. tolaintatum, taṉakkuriya āṭci iṉṉum varavillaiyē eṉṟu toḻilāḷi niṉaikkumāṟu kāṅkira āṭci itu pōtu naṭakkiṟatu. kāṅkira cuyaāṭci taṉṉaik kāy vataiyum, aṭakkuvataiyum, taṉṉurimaiyaik kaṭiyak koṭuñ caṭṭaṅkaḷ niṟaivēṟṟuvataiyum, vicāraṇai yillāmal tan talaivarkaḷ ciṟaikku aṉuppappaṭuvataiyum. ï‹D« ãwt‰iwí« bjhÊ yhË cz®»wh‹.: cintaṉaiyil āḻkiṟāṉ. avaṉ uḷḷattil eṉṉeṉṉavō eḻukiṉṟaṉa? innilaiyil kāṅkira eṉ ceytal vēṇṭum?
kāṅkira anniya āṭciyiṉiṉṟum viṭutalai aṭaintatu. iṉi atu maṟaital vēṇṭum; allatu muḻu viṭutalaikku muyalutal vēṇṭum. iraṇṭil oṉṟu kāṅkira cārpil nikaḻa vēṇṭumeṉṟu nāṭu etir pārkkiṟatu.
kāṅkira talaivarkaḷiṟ cilar kāṅkira maṟaivai virumpukiṟārillai. avar māṟutalil nāṭṭañ celuttukiṟār. māṟṟam pōṟṟaṟkuriyatē. āṉāl, māṟṟam emmuṟaiyil nikaḻvatu nallatu? camatarmamuṟaiyil māṟṟam nikaḻvatē nallatu. kāṅkirasai itu pōḻtu taṉ vayappaṭuttiyuḷḷa mutalāḷi immāṟṟattukku iṇaṅkuvaṉō? itu paṟṟiya karuttu vēṟṟumai kāṅkira talaivarkaḷiṉ mūḷaiyaik kalakkik koṇṭirukkiṟatu. iyaṟkai yiṉ nōkkukkum pōkkukkum araṇ ceyyum vaḻiniṉṟu kāṅkira kaṭaṉāṟṟuvatē aṟam. makātmā kānti iyaṟkai vaḻiniṉṟu nalvaḻi kāṭṭuvāreṉṟu nāṭu nampukiṟatu. paṇṭita javaharlāl nēruvaiyum nāṭu nōkkukiṟatu.
mutal - toḻil orumaippaṭak kāṅkira muyalvatu kālattukkuriya toṇṭākum. iṉi, mutal - toḻil eṉṉum īriṉam vāḻvu peṟutaṟku iṭamillai eṉṟu iyaṟkai eccarikkai ceyta vaṇṇamirukkiṟatu. ātaliṉ, īriṉattai ōriṉamākka muyalvatu iyaṟkaiyiṉ nōkkukkum pōkkukkum tuṇai ceyva tākum.
ātināḷil mutal - toḻil eṉṟa īriṉam aṟṟa camūkamē tikaḻntatu. piṉṉē atu tirintu tirintu ippoḻutu mutal - toḻil eṉṉum īriṉamākiyatu. īriṉam paḻaiyapaṭi ōriṉamāṉā laṉṟi ulakil amaiti nilavātu.
vitaiyiṉiṉṟum muḷai eḻukiṟatu. muḷai ceṭiyākiṟatu. ceṭi maramākiṟatu. maram ñāyiṟākiṟatō? tiṅkaḷākiṟatō? illai. piṉṉai eṉṉa ākiṟatu? maram kaṉi viṭukiṟatu. kaṉiyi ṉiṉṟum paḻaiya vitaiyē tōṉṟukiṟatu. atē pōlac camūkam īriṉam aṟṟa oṉṟiṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiyatu. atu mīṇṭum paḻaiya nilaiyaiyē eytum; eytiyē tīrum, iḵtu iyaṟkai. iyaṟkaikkut tuṇaipōvatē aṟam - ahimsai.
īriṉam eḷitil ōriṉamākumā? āyiṉāl nallatē. illaiyēl puraṭci eḻum puraṭci aṟavaḻi nikaḻtal vēṇṭum. ekkāraṇam paṟṟiyum puraṭci matavaḻi nikaḻtalākātu. kāntiyam namakku nalvaḻi kāṭṭiyuḷḷatu. aḵtu anniya āṭciyai eppaṭit tolaittatu? ahimsaiyālaṉṟō tolaittatu? anta ahimsaiyaiyē īriṉattai ōriṉamākkuvataṟkuṅ kōṭal poruttam. ahimsaiyē intiyat tarumam. ataṟkuk kēṭu cūḻalākātu.
īriṉam oḻinta camatarumattai innāḷil viññāṉa muṟaiyil aṟivuṟuttiyavar kāral mārk. ammārksiyamum nam kāntiyamum oṉṟik kaṭaṉāṟṟiṉāl intiyat tarumattaik kāttal kūṭum. iraṇṭuṅkalanta oṉṟē namatu nāṭṭukkut tēvai eṉṟu pala āṇṭāka yāṉ valiyuṟutti varuvatu nāṭṭavarkkut teriyum.
mārksiyamum kāntiyamum cērnta oṉṟaiyē yāṉ caṉmārkkam eṉṟu pēciyum eḻutiyum varukiṟēṉ. catteṉuñ cemporuḷai uṇarttuvatu kāntiyameṉṟum, atai aṭaiyum mārkkattaik kāṭṭuvatu mārksiyameṉṟuṅ koḷka. muḻu viṭutalaikku - poruḷātāra viṭutalaikku - camatarmattukkuc ceyyappaṭum muyaṟciyiṉaṭiyil ahimsai vērkoṇṭu niṟṟal avaciyam eṉṟu aṟaikūvukiṟēṉ.
tiṭṭam
camatarmattaik kuṟikkōḷāk koṇṭu kāṅkira cila tiṭṭaṅkaḷaik kōlum muyaṟciyil talaippaṭuvatu ciṟappu. namatu nāṭu nīṇṭakālamāka anniya āṭcikku iraiyāṉamaiyāl, atu nāṭṭukkuriya kalaikaḷuṭaṉ peritun toṭarpu koḷḷā toḻintatu. nāṭṭuk kalaikaḷ nāṭṭil mīṇṭum uyirt teḻuntāl camatarmam makkaṭkup putumaiyākat tōṉṟātu; atu paḻamaiyākavē tōṉṟum. intiyak kalaiñāṉam iṭaiyil piṟaḻntupaṭṭamaiyāl nāṭṭil cātimatap piṇakkukaḷ nāṭṭaip palavaḻiyilum alaittaṉa; arittaṉa; erittaṉa. av valaivu, arippu, erivu māyntu pōka uḻaittal vēṇṭum. cātimatak kuṟumpukaḷait tolaippataṟkup pala vaḻi kūṟalām. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟaic ciṟappāka muṉṉaruṅ kūṟiṉēṉ. īṇṭuṅ kuṟikkiṟēṉ. atu kalappumaṇam. kalappu maṇattāl cātimatap puṉmai poṉṟuvatu tiṇṇam. 
nāṭṭu vaḷarccikkuk karuttu vēṟṟumai iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. karuttu vēṟṟumaikku iṭantārāta nāṭu vaḷarcciyuṟātu ciṟaippaṭṭuc cāmpum. karuttu vēṟṟumaiyaik kāyātu kaṭiyātu ataṟku iṭantaruvatu vaḷarcciyai nāṭuvatākum. namatu nāṭṭil muṉṉāḷil naṉṉeṟikaḷ pala vaḷarntē vantaṉa. vaḷarccikkuk kāraṇam annāḷil karuttu vēṟṟumaikku iṭantantamaiyē yākum.  ivvaṟa voḻukkam iṭaikkālattil vīḻntatu. ataṉāl nāṭu kālattukkēṟṟa vaḷarcci peṟāmal tēṅkiyatu. iṭaik kālattil iṟaṅkiya nañcu - iṉṉum ivvirupatām nūṟṟāṇṭi lum - pala nāṭukaḷiṉ kūṭṭuṟavu peṟṟa innāḷilum - anniya āṭciyāl kaṭṭuṇṭu oruvita viṭutalaipeṟṟa ivvēḷaiyilum - oḻiyavillai. karuttu vēṟṟumai kāraṇamāka oruvarai oruvar pakaittal aṟiyāmai; iḻivu; nīcam; caṇṭāḷam. karuttu vēṟṟumaikku iṭantaruvatu camatarma vaḷarccikku iṭantaruvatākum. karuttu vēṟṟumaikku iṭantarum aṟivukkalai vaḷarvataṟkut talaivarkaḷ naṭantu nalvaḻi kāṭṭi uḻaikka muṟpaṭuvārkaḷāka.
jaṉanāyaka āṭci makkaḷiṉ vākkiṉiṉṟum eḻuntu amaivatu āṭcikku mūlamāyiruppatu vākku, attakaiya vākku ettakaiya poṟuppuṭaiyatā yiruttal vēṇṭum? itai maṉpatai uṇarntu naṭakkuṅ kālamē aṟavaḷarccik kuriyatākum.
vākku, tūya urimaiyiṉiṉṟum eḻum teyvīkamuṭaiyatu. atait teyvakkoṭai eṉṟuṅ koḷḷalām. av vākkai nalvaḻiyil celutta vēṇṭuvatu makkaḷ kaṭamai. atu vēṟu vaḻiyil celuttappaṭiṉ, jaṉanāyakam pōliyāy, nañcāy nāṭṭai erippatākum.
namatu intiya nāṭu toṉmai vāyntatu; carittira kālattukku muṉṉarē nākarikattiṟ paṇpaṭṭu viḷaṅkiyatu. malaiyiṉ māṇpaiyum kaṭaliṉ ciṟappaiyum uṇarntatu; kāviya ōviyaṅ kaḷaik kaṇṭatu; vāḻkkait tattuvattil teḷivu peṟṟatu. ippeṟṟi vāynta nāṭu iṭaikkālattil cātimata vēṟṟumai kaḷāl pala tiṟak kuḻappaṅkaṭku iraiyākiyatu. ataṉāl atu nāḷukkunāḷ aṭimaiyākiyākit taṉatu nalliyalpukaḷai yellām iḻantatu.
ēṟakkuṟaiya irunūṟāṇṭu namatu intiya nāṭu piriṭṭiṣā rāl āḷappaṭṭatu; avartam nākarika vaḻiyē araciyal payiṉṟatu; jaṉanāyakattai vēṭṭatu. itu pōḻtu jaṉanāyakattukkuriya oruvita viṭutalaiyaiyum peṟṟatu. ivviṭutalaiyai muḻumaiya tākkum poṟuppu nāṭṭiṉ muṉṉē niṟkiṟatu.
muḻu viṭutalaikkup palappala iyalpukaḷ tēvai. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu vākkurimaiyai nalvaḻiyiṟ celuttuvatu. ivviyalpu namatu nāṭṭil ennilaiyil uḷḷatu? itaṟku viṭai iṟukkavallatu anupavam, anupavam iṉṉateṉpataic caṭṭa capaikaḷ aṟivuṟuttā niṟkiṉṟaṉa.
namatu nāṭṭil peritum cātimata veṟikaḷum, paṇam, tayai tāṭcaṇyam mutaliya māyaikaḷum vākkāyp pariṇamittuc caṭṭacapaikaḷātal veḷḷiṭaimalai, pōtiya kalviyaṟivu, araciyal ñāṉam mutaliyaṉa vāykkap peṟāta makkaḷ vākku eṅṅaṉam urimaiyuṇarviṉiṉṟum eḻum?
innāḷil vayatuṟṟavarellārukkum vākkurimai kiṭait tuḷḷatu. itu pōṟṟaṟkuriyatē. āṉāl vākkurimai nāṭṭil cenneṟiyil celuttappaṭumā eṉṟu aṟiñar ulakam aiyuṟukiṟatu. curuṅkiya muṟaiyil muṉṉē kiṭaitta vākkurimai cenneṟiyil celuttappaṭāmaiyē aiyattukkuk kāraṇamāy niṟkiṟatu pōlum!
vākkāḷar cintai araciyaṟ potumaiyiṟ cellumāṟum, cāti matam paṇam tayai tāṭcaṇyam mutaliya māyaikaḷil cellātavāṟum kākka valla kalviyum araciyal ñāṉamum avarkkut tēvai. ittēvaiyai niṟaivu ceyyak kāṅkira muyalvatu nallatu. vākkāḷar maṉōnilai cīrpaṭak kāṅkira piracārañ ceyvatāka; āṅkāṅkē araciyaṟpaḷḷi amaittu makkaḷukkup pōtaṉai ceyvatāka. kāṅkira taṉ kaṭaṉai ittuṟaiyil āṟṟātoḻiyiṉ, caṭṭacapaikaḷil tēḷ pāmpu karaṭi, puli mutaliyaṉa ulavutal nērum. ākavē, kāṅkira taṉ poṟuppai uṇarntu naṭappatāka.
makkaḷāṭci vaḷartaṟku orēvita araciyal kaṭci nilavutal kūṭātu. atu pāsisamāy nāṭṭai iṭarppaṭuttum; jaṉanāya kattaik kollum. ākavē, palatiṟak karuttuṭaiya araciyal kaṭcikaḷ amaivatē ciṟappu. cāti mataṅkaḷai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭa kaṭcikaḷiṉ tōṟṟam, cātimataṅkaṭkeṉṟu pīṭaṅkaḷai otukkal mutaliyaṉa nāṭṭaic ciṟumaip paṭuttum: ciṟaippaṭuttum. cātimatam mutaliya ciṟumaikaḷaṟṟa kaṭcikaḷē nāṭṭai vaḷarkkum. nāṭṭiṉ vaḷarccikku itukāṟum pāṭupaṭṭu oruvita viṭutalaiyai nāṭṭukku nalkiya kāṅkira, iṉi nalla araciyal kaṭcikaḷ amaiyavum tuṇai ceyvatāka; kāṅkira oru kaṭciyāka niṉṟu kaṭaṉāṟṟātiruppatāka.
ikkāla nākarikak kuḻavikaḷ cila. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu pattirikai! intiyap pattirikaiyulakam iṉṉum naṉmuṟaiyil paṇpaṭavillai. intiyap pattirikai ulakam peritum vakuppu vātattukku ūṟṟāka niṟṟal veḷḷiṭaimalai nāṭṭil viḷaintuvarum vakuppuppūcal - piṇakku - pōr - kolai - koḷḷai mutaliya koṭumaikaṭkellām mūla kāraṇaṅkaḷāka niṟpaṉavaṟṟuḷ pattirikai ulakamē ciṟantu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. anniya āṭcivilakiya nāḷtoṭṭu, nāṭṭup pattirikaikaḷiṟ perum pāṉmaiyaṉa, mutalāḷiyaip pōṟṟiyum toḻilāḷiyait tūṟṟiyum eḻutat toṭaṅkiṉa; kāṅkira āṭci cārpil nikaḻun tavaṟutalkaḷaik kaṭṭuppāṭāka maṟaittu varukiṉṟaṉa. taṉittaṉi maṉitarait tākkutal, poymaikaḷaip parapputal, vacaimoḻi kaḷaic corital, vakuppup pōrkaḷait tūṇṭal mutaliya ciṟumaikaḷ malintu miṭaiyum pattirikaip perukkaliṉāl nāṭu naṉṉilai eytumā? intiyap pattirikaiyulakam camatarmattai ātarikka uṟutikoḷḷutal vēṇṭum; koṇṭāl pala tuṟaikaḷ cīrpaṭum. pattirikaic cīrtiruttattukkeṉṟu oru taṉi iyakkam tōṉṟiṉum atai nāṭu varavēṟkum.
kāṅkira āṭci aṭakkumuṟaic caṭṭaṅkaḷai oḻikka iyalātu, putupputu aṭakkumuṟaic caṭṭaṅkaḷai niṟuva muyalvatu, muḻu viṭutalaiyaik kuṟikkoṇṭu uḻaippatākātu. aṭakku muṟaic caṭṭaṅkaḷ kolai kaḷavu poy mutaliyavaṟṟai vaḷarkkum maṟakkaruvikaḷ, koṭuñ caṭṭaṅkaḷāl nāṭṭai arippatu nākarikamākātu. accaṭṭaṅkaḷ nākarika makkaḷaiyum anākarika mākkaḷākkum. kāṅkira āṭci caṭṭaṅkaḷaic curukkic curukki aṟattaip perukka muṉaivatu camatarmattukkuk kāl koḷvatākum.
reyil, ṭirām, pa, ciṉimā, pāṅk, toḻiṟ cālaikaḷ mutaliyaṉa taṉi maṉitar vayappaṭāmal, potumai araciṉ vayappaṭumāṟum, avvāṟē nilapulaṅkaḷum, piṟavum potumaiyākumāṟum kāṅkira tiṭṭaṅ kōlic camatarmattai vaḷarkka eḻuvatāka. ellām camatarma mayamāṉāl pōlī, paṭṭāḷam, kolaik karuvikaḷ mutaliyaṉa eṟṟukku? mutalil kāṅkira camatarma vaḻiyil māṟṟam peṟat talaivarkaḷ toṇṭāṟṟuvārkaḷāka. intiyāvil camatarmam, mārksiya - kāntiya vaḻiyē eḻuvatāka, etaṟkum puraṭci tēvai. puraṭci aṟavaḻi nikaḻvatāka. maṟappuraṭci vēṇṭā; vēṇṭā; ekkāraṇam paṟṟiyum vēṇṭā. kāṅkira camatarmattaik kaṭaippiṭittoḻuka āṇṭavaṉ aruḷ ceyvāṉāka. ellārum iṉpuṟ ṟirukka niṉaippatuvē - allāmal vēṟoṉ ṟaṟiyēṉ parāparamē eṉpatu tāyumāṉār tiruvākku.

muṭivurai
innūlil mūvakai intiyāvum mūvakai rājyamum pēcappaṭ ṭuḷḷaṉa; muṟkāla intiyāvum iṭaikkāla intiyāvum, hintu rājyamum mulim rājyamum eppaṭi vaḷarntu tēyntaṉa eṉpatum, ikkāla intiyā ekkāṭci vaḻaṅkukiṟatu eṉpatum, intiyāvil piriṭṭil āṭci evvāṟu nuḻaintatu eṉpatum viritturaikkap paṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. nāṭṭai aṭimaippaṭutti yavai iyaṟkaikku māṟupaṭṭa taṉi vāḻkkai, piṟappil uyarvu tāḻvu karutal, mataveṟi, peṇṇaṭimai, tīṇṭāmai, kaṇmūṭi vaḻakka oḻukkac campiratāyaṅkaḷ, cakōtara nēyamiṉmai, kāṭṭik koṭuttal, hintu - mulim vēṟṟumai mutaliyaṉa eṉṟum, ikkuṟaipāṭukaḷ anniya āṭciyaik kūvi aḻaittaṉa eṉṟum, ippoḻutu kiṭaittuḷḷa viṭutalai muḻumaiyatākātu eṉṟum, iṉip poruḷātāra viṭutalai muyaṟci virainteḻal vēṇṭum eṉṟum, ammuyaṟciyilēyē peruṅkavalai celuttal vēṇṭum  eṉṟum, mutalil kāṅkira camatarma mayamātal vēṇṭum eṉṟum viḷakkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
muḻuviṭutalai muyaṟci eṉṟatum pirañcup puraṭci ruṣyap puraṭci mutaliya maṟappuraṭcikaḷ mītu cilar eṇṇañ cellum. maṟappuraṭci pōrai vaḷarppatu; tēyppataṉṟu. veṭṭukiṟavaṉ veṭṭappaṭuvāṉ eṉpatu kiṟitu perumāṉ perumoḻi, piṉṉai etu nallatu? aṟappuraṭciyē nallatu. intiya muṉivarāl aṟivuṟuttappeṟṟatu aṟappuraṭciyē.
eṇṇam
aṟappuraṭcikkuk kolaik karuvikaḷ tēvai illai. ataṟku nalliyalpukaḷ tēvai. nalliyalpukaḷ amaitaṟkup palappala muṟaikaḷ aṟavōrāl kōlappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟai aṟanūlkaḷiṟ pārkka. īṇṭu oṉṟaic ciṟappāka valiyuṟutta virumpukiṟēṉ.
emmuyaṟcikkum aṭippaṭai eṇṇam. eṇṇam pēccā kiṟatu; ceyalākiṟatu. eṇṇam iruvakaiyatu. oruvakai, puṟamaṉatti ṉiṉṟum piṟappatu; maṟṟoru vakai, uḷmaṉattiṉiṉṟum piṟappatu. iraṇṭuḷ piṉṉaiyatē ciṟantatu.
puṟa (maṉa) eṇṇamum, ataṉiṉṟeḻum pēccum ceyalum taṉṉalattai vaḷarppaṉavāy, mūrkka caktiyai eḻuppuvaṉavāyp pōrāṭṭattaip perukkuvaṉavām. uḷ (maṉa) eṇṇam pēccākavum ceyalākavuṅkūṭa eḻa vēṇṭuvatillai. av veṇṇamē āṟṟal vāyntatu; avvāṟṟal aḷapparitu. atu taṉṉalaṅ kaṭantatu; āṉmacakti vāyntatu. pīraṅki kuṇṭuka ḷālum accaktiyai aḻittal iyalātu.
tavam
uḷ uṇarvāl oṉṟai eṇṇi uṉṉi muṉṉuvatu āḻnta cintaṉai allatu tavam eṉṉappaṭum. maṉaivi makkaḷai valintu nīttuk kāṭṭukkōṭi mūkkaip piṭittu mūccai aṭakkuvatu tavamākātu. tavamāvatu, akavuṇarvaip paṇpaṭutti, atil oṉṟi āḻnta cintaṉaiyil amarvatu. itu cintaṉākāram. ippayiṟciyil nammuṉṉōr mikattērcci peṟṟavarāyiruntaṉar. ataṟkeṉṟē avartaṅ kalvi, celvam, toḻil, uṇavu, uṭai, vāḻkkai, aracu mutaliyaṉa payaṉpaṭṭu vantaṉa. innāḷil akavuṇarvu nuṭpam mēlnāṭṭil akattiṇaiyar (Psychologists) cilariṭam viḷaṅki varukiṟatu. atu nāḷaṭaivil aṅkē ākkam peṟalām.
āḻnta cintaṉaikkup puṟa maṉa oṭukkam vēṇṭum. ataṟkup pulaṉaṭakkam iṉṟiyamaiyātatu. pulaṉaṭakkattukku nalloḻukkam tēvai. nalloḻukkam eḷiya aṟa vāḻkkaiyil arumpum.
1pulaṉaṭakkam piṉṉāḷil pulaṉaḻivākak koḷḷappaṭṭatu. itu tiripu. itai, peṇṇiṉ perumai, murukaṉ allatu aḻaku mutaliya nūlkaḷil viḷakkiyuḷḷēṉ. 
eḷiya aṟa vāḻkkai viḻumiya nōkkattai vaḷarkka vallatu, eḷiya vāḻkkaiyum viḻumiya nōkkamum namatu nāṭṭuc celvam, accelvam eṅkē? pērācaip pēy vāḻkkaiyum taṉṉala nōkkamumallavō innāḷil nāṭṭai arikkiṉṟaṉa?
eḷiya vāḻkkaiyāl pala nalaṉkaḷ uṇṭu. avaṟṟuḷ ciṟantaṉa cutēciya ākkamum ahimsai nōkkamumākum. iraṇṭum meymmai viṭutalaikku iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa. avaṟṟaik koṇṭu, makātmā kānti periya cāmrājyattai etirttu, aṟappōr purintu, oruvita viṭutalai peṟṟatu ulakaṟintatoṉṟu. ahimsai aṟṟa iṭattil maṟappōr - maṟappuraṭci - nikaḻntu koṇṭē pōkum.
intiya muḻu viṭutalaikku - camatarma āṭcikku - aṟap puraṭciyē cālacciṟantatu. appuraṭciyāl muṟkāla intiyā taṟkālap pōrvaiyuṭaṉ uyirtteḻum. avvintiyā, himsai mayamāka uḷḷa ikkāla ulaka viṭutalaikkum vaḻi kāṭṭuvatākum. ātalāl, mutalil vēṇṭaṟpālatu intiya muḻu viṭutalai atāvatu camatarmam. avviṭutalai vēṭkaiyai eḻuppuvatu innūl.
tiru. vi. kaliyāṇacuntaraṉ


1. jamputtīpam (jampu-nāval)

1.	ēḻu peruntīviṉ naṭuttīvāvatu nūṟāyiram yōcaṉaip parappuṭaiyatāy vaṭṭamāy nilamakaṭku untittāṉamāyuḷḷa nāvalantīvu... itu mirukēntirattiṟ kaṇṭatu. - civañāṉapāṭiyam
2.	17.62
1.	āraṅ kaṇṭikai āṭaiyuṅ kantaiyē
pāram īcaṉ paṇiala toṉṟilār
īra aṉpiṉar yātuṅ kuṟaivilār 
vīram eṉṉāl viḷampun takaiyatō 	- cēkkiḻār

1.	ātimanu jainar eṉṟu jaina nūlkaḷ colkiṉṟaṉa; tirāviṭareṉṟa campiratāyamum uṇṭu.
2.	jainam taṉ koḷkaiyait tarmam eṉṟē kūṟukiṟatu.

1.	‘Rivers, water - courses, running through the village, ponds large and small, burning - places, were free to all. Every village had a comparatively large space of land round it for pasture. This, as well as lands which were entirely barren and uncultivable, were also common property On lands which were not cultivated any villager had the right to graze his cattle. On lands which were cultivated after the harvest. Before the next cultivating season, the same right of grazing existed. Everybody had an equal right to cut jungle fire 	- wood for fuel, and take jungle produce for manure, and to take timber for building his house. This right was only limited and generally regulated by the village assembly. The rights in cultivable lands varied with the type of village. The village, or the rights in the village, were vested in the settlers as a collective body.*** By this means they effectully excluded the right of any proprietor to any particular spot and established the claim of the general body to the Whole village in common’	- T.Rangachari

1. 	iṟanta  kālan teṇṇilput tarkaḷum
 ciṟantaruḷ kūrntu tiruvāy moḻinta 	     - maṇimēkalai 30-14-15
	kautama puttaruṭaiya kālattiṟku muṉpu tarumaṅ kuṟainta ovvoru kālattum ovvoruvarākap puttarkaḷ palar avatarittu upatēcañceytu taruma tāpaṉañ ceytār kaḷeṉpatu pautta nūṟṟuṇipātaliṉ, iṟanta kālat teṇṇilput tarkaḷum eṉṟār; kautama puttaruṭaiya carittirattilum, putta jātakak kataiyilum ikkaruttu āṅkāṅkup peṟappaṭum; kautama puttarukku muṉṉēyuḷḷa puttarkaḷuḷ, irupatteḻuvar peyarkaḷ maṭṭum ippoḻutu terikiṉṟaṉa. avai varumāṟu : 	
	(1) taṇṇakārar (2) mētaṅkarar (3) caraṇaṅkarar (4) tīpaṅkarar (5) kauṇṭiṉṉiyar 
(6) maṅkaḷar (7) sīmaṅkaḷar (8) irēvatar (9) cōpitar (10) anōmatarci  (11) patumar 
(12) nāratar (13) patumōttarar (14) sumētar (15)sujātar (16) piriyatarci (17) arttatarci (18) tarmatarci (19) sittārttar (20) tiṣyar (21) puṣyar (22) vipaccit (23) ciki 
(24) viruṣapar (25) kakuntar (26) kōvakāmar (27) kaciyapar. ivvirupatteḻuvarum kautamaputtarum sarvajña puttareṉṟu kūṟappaṭuvar. iḵtu, ilaṅkaiyiluḷḷa vittiyōtaya pāṭacālait talaivarākiya srī sumaṅkaḷarāl teriyavantatu. taṇṇaṅkāraṉ, mahāvīraṉ mutaliya irupattu mūṉṟu putta reṉpārum uḷar.			- ṭākṭar cāmināta aiyar

1.	meyyo ṭēpoyyum viraviṭu māṟupaṉ ṉūluṭ
kaiya rāyavar palanuḻait tāravai kaṇittē
aiya nīkkuta laritari tēyata ṉāliv
vaiya nāṭuva rōvuri yavarayiṉ mataṉē.
    	poyyil vētavi yācaṉa turaiyeṉum purāṇat 
teyvam vāḻtal mīteṉap palakatai terittē
uyya lāmauc ceytacu vaṭikaḷu muḷavāṉ
meyya riṅkuṟal viḻaivarko lōvayil vēntē.

1.  toḻilāḷar caṅkaṅkaḷ āṅkāṅkē nāṭōṟun tōṉṟi varukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟai vaḻi tuṟaiyillāmal vaḷarttu varuvatāl, pala iṭarkaḷ iṭaiyil nērntuviṭum. ātalāl, ellāt toḻiṟ caṅkaṅkaṭkum naṭu nāyakamāka oru mattiyac caṅkamiruttal vēṇṭum. accaṅkattil ellāt toḻiṟ caṅkaṅkaḷuñ cērtal vēṇṭum. mattiyac caṅkam oṉṟiruppatu toḻilāḷar iyakkattukku ākkantēṭuvatākum. oru mattiyac caṅkam amaikka srīmati mirunāḷiṉi caṭōpāttiyāyā ceytuvarum muyaṟcikku immakānāṭu tuṇai ceyyumeṉṟu nampukiṟēṉ.
1. 	To
	KALYANASUNDARA MUDALIYAR, ESQ. Madras.
My dear friend,
 	This is your book as much as mine. If the speeches delivered here achieved any good among our friends at Perambur it is due to your excellent
	translations of them. What could I have done in the Labour work in this city without you? You translated my speeches - not my words merely but their very spirit. We have rejoiced and suffered together with thousands. Your unobtrusive manner, your loyal support. Your sagacious advice have been priceless. Let me request you to accept this small gift of dedication for your all too splendid services. 			Ever yours, 				B.P. WADIA.,
(‘Labour in Madras’ by B.P.Wadia with a foreword by Col. Josiah C. Wedg-wood. M.P., (1921). S’ Ganesan Co., Publishers, Triplicane, Madras, S.E.)
	“.......All these parties were present on the 10th August except the Madras Labour Union President who addressed the Committee the following letter:-
	Sir,
	I have the honour to acknowledge the receipt of your letter dated the 6th inst. relating to the sittings of the committee of inquiry into the Madras Disturbances of June and July 1921 and to thank you for inviting me to be present at the said inquiry.
	In reply I am desired by the Madras Labour Union to inform you that the Madras Labour Union as such does not propose to participate in the inquiry which as the Government order points out, will not extend to the merits and to any disputes between the managements of the Buckingham and Carnatic Mills and their employees. The inquiry into the disturbances which took place in the Perambur division on and after the 29th June, is a matter of concern to the general public and not to the Members of the Union as such, and it affects the peace and order of northern parts of the city. The Labour Union repudiates any manner of connection with such disturbances as have taken place. They have since been in part at least a matter of adjudication before the Magisterial Courts in Madras and evidence has been adduced before these courts at the instance of the police as well as the parties aggrieved and judgements have been pronounced by the Chief Presidency Magistrate of the city in two cases which show clearly the origin of the disturbance and the persons involved in provoking them as well as the conduct of the police in dealing with them. There are still a number of police proceedings pending before stipendiary and honorary magistrates in respect of this matter, in which Members of the Union are being needlessly harassed and put to great hardship. In the circumstances,
	the Union is unable to furnish the Committee with a list of persons who might be examined as wittnesses by your committee and I regret very much to find myself unable to supply you now or later with a list of persons whom I could in the existing state of things suggest for examination as witnesses.
		Yours truly,
	T.V.KALYANASUNDARAM
	President, Madras Labour Union.
	   In consequence of this refusal on the part of the Labour Union to participate in the inquiry which was much regretted by the committee, the committee announced at the first meeting that they desired it to be known that any accredited representatives of the strikers would be affored the same facilities in regard to this inquiry as would have been given to the Madras Labour Union. The representatives of the Press were asked to give every possible publicity to this announcement. But unfortunately no representative of the strikers or any section of them appeared during the inquiry......”			- “Hindu” (14-10-1921)
(G.O.No.671, 7th October 1921, Madras Disturbances july - 1921)
